=== PROMPT ===
Here is some python code and the inputs passed into it.
Determine the output by simulating the execution.

Code: python
def _get_part(self, s, decimal):
        "Strips first part of string containing either non-decimal or decimal characters." \
        + " Returns tuple (part, remider)."
        div = 0
        for c in s:
            if decimal and not c.isdecimal():
                break
            elif not decimal and c.isdecimal():
                break
            else:
                div += 1

        return (s[:div], s[div:])
Inputs: 2.2.0~rc5, '2.2.0~rc5', False, 0, '0', ('.', '+', '~'), '2.2.0~rc5', ('.', '+', '~', '-', ':'), '2.2.0~rc5'

Reason in a direct trace-first way.

A productive pattern for this task usually looks like this:
- Identify the true entry path, the incoming input state, and the first statement that actually runs.
- Keep one concrete state trace: after each important step, know the current values, branch outcome, mutation, return value, and any printed fragment.
- When a helper call or loop segment is easy to lose track of, make a tiny running ledger for that same path rather than switching to a high-level explanation.
- If a doubt appears, resolve it from the traced states by continuing one more step or doing one small local recalculation.
- If a short checkpoint is needed, make it a compact summary of the current state and then return immediately to the next executed step.
- Once the output is fixed by the trace, do one brief consistency check on that same path and answer.

Answer using <ans></ans> tags only.

Function `_get_part(self, s, decimal)` "Strips first part of string containing either non-decimal or decimal characters." Returns tuple (part, remider). It loops over chars, if decimal True and char not decimal -> break; elif decimal False and char decimal -> break; else div += 1. So if decimal=True, it consumes consecutive decimal digits until first non-digit, returns (digits, rest). If decimal=False, it consumes consecutive non-digit chars until first digit, returns (non-digits, rest).

    epoch = None
    release = []
    pre = None
    post = None
    dev = None

    if "!" in version:
        epoch, version = version.split("!", 1)
        if not epoch.isdigit():
            raise InvalidVersion(...)
        epoch = int(epoch)

    # Parse the release

        if not part:

        if any(c.isdigit() for c in part):

def _get_part(self, s, decimal):
    "Strips first part of string containing either non-decimal or decimal characters."
    div = 0
    for c in s:
        if decimal and not c.isdecimal():
            break
        elif not decimal and c.isdecimal():
            break
        else:
            div += 1
    return (s[:div], s[div:])
```

```
VERSION_PATTERN = r"""
    v?
    (?:
        (?:(?P<epoch>[0-9]+)!)?                           # epoch
        (?P<release>[0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+)*)                  # release segment
        (?P<pre>                                          # pre-release
            [-_\.]?
            (?P<pre_l>alpha|a|beta|b|preview|pre|c|rc)
            [-_\.]?
            (?P<pre_n>[0-9]+)?
        )?
        (?P<post>                                         # post release
            (?:-(?P<post_n1>[0-9]+))
            |
            (?:
                [-_\.]?
                (?P<post_l>post|rev|r)
                [-_\.]?
                (?P<post_n2>[0-9]+)?
            )
        )?
        (?P<dev>                                          # dev release
            [-_\.]?
            (?P<dev_l>dev)
            [-_\.]?
            (?P<dev_n>[0-9]+)?
        )?
    )
    (?:\+(?P<local>[a-z0-9]+(?:[-_\.][a-z0-9]+)*))?
"""
```
